# IMPERIA COLD WAR — USA and Soviet political leadership integration.
COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_choose_1_richard_nixon_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				original_tag = USA
				exists = yes
				is_dynamic_country = no
				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Richard Nixon" ruling_only = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Richard Nixon" ruling_only = yes } }
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Richard Nixon"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/USA/USA_Coldwar_Nixon.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_richard_nixon_desc
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = { career_politician }
			}
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_observe = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SP_1970_note_USA_richard_nixon_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = USA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_observe = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_choose_2_hubert_humphrey_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				original_tag = USA
				exists = yes
				is_dynamic_country = no
				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
			}
			western_liberals_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Hubert Humphrey" ruling_only = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_2 = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Hubert Humphrey" ruling_only = yes } }
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Hubert Humphrey"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/USA_hubert_humphrey.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_hubert_humphrey_desc
				ideology = liberalism
			}
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_observe = yes
	}
}

				is_ai = no
			}

COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_set_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_supported = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_observed_party = ruling_party }
			}
			COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_observe = yes
		}
		update_set_politics = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { MDCW_HP_native_retirement = 1 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 > 13 }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Richard Nixon" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Richard Nixon"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/USA/USA_Coldwar_Nixon.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_richard_nixon_desc
						ideology = conservatism
						traits = { career_politician }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_USA_preference_1 = 2 }

COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_choose_23_vladimir_kirillovich_effect = {

		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {

				exists = yes

				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
			}
			nationalist_monarchists_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { OR = {
				has_country_leader = { name = "Grand Duke Vladimir Kirillovich" ruling_only = yes }
				has_country_leader = { name = "Emperor Vladimir III" ruling_only = yes }
				has_country_leader = { name = "Vladimir Kirillovich" ruling_only = yes }
			} }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_23 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_SOV_clear_heirs = yes
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_bre_retiring
		MDCW_HP_SOV_clear_manual_retirement_pending = yes
		if = {

				NOT = { OR = {
					has_country_leader = { name = "Grand Duke Vladimir Kirillovich" ruling_only = yes }
					has_country_leader = { name = "Emperor Vladimir III" ruling_only = yes }
					has_country_leader = { name = "Vladimir Kirillovich" ruling_only = yes }
				} }
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Grand Duke Vladimir Kirillovich"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/SOV_vladimir_kirillovich.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_vladimir_kirillovich_desc
				ideology = Monarchist
			}
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes

	}

COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_observe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = SOV
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = { name = "Dzhermen Gvishiani" ruling_only = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_bre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_kos
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_pod
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_sus
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_she
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_and
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gre
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_ust
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gro
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_che
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gor
						}
					}
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_bre
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_kos
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_pod
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_sus
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_she
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_and
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gre
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_ust
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gro
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_che
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_gor
					force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = { name = "Vladimir Poremsky" ruling_only = yes }

							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_she
							has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_and

					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SOV_gensec_pod

COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_set_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_supported = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_observed_party = ruling_party }
			}
			COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_observe = yes
		}
		update_set_politics = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { MDCW_HP_native_retirement = 1 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 > 1 }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Dzhermen Gvishiani" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Dzhermen Gvishiani"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/SOV_dzhermen_gvishiani.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_dzhermen_gvishiani_desc
						ideology = Western_Autocracy
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_0 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Dzhermen Gvishiani" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Dzhermen Gvishiani"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/SOV_dzhermen_gvishiani.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_dzhermen_gvishiani_desc
						ideology = Western_Autocracy
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { MDCW_HP_native_retirement = 1 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_1 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_1 > 1 }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_1 = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_1 = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Vladimir Poremsky" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Vladimir Poremsky"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/SOV_vladimir_poremsky.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_vladimir_poremsky_desc
						ideology = conservatism
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_preference_1 = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Vladimir Poremsky" ruling_only = yes } }
					}
					retire_country_leader = yes
					create_country_leader = {
						name = "Vladimir Poremsky"
						picture = "gfx/leaders/COLDWAR_superpower_1970/SOV_vladimir_poremsky.dds"
						desc = COLDWAR_SP_1970_SOV_vladimir_poremsky_desc
						ideology = conservatism
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				western_liberals_are_in_power = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { MDCW_HP_native_retirement = 1 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire }
